value.bind(client) : value\n    44→  },\n    45→})\n    46→\n    47→/**\n    48→ * Send order confirmation email\n    49→ */\n    50→export async function sendOrderConfirmationEmail(params: {\n    51→  to: string\n    52→  orderId: string\n    53→  orderNumber: string\n    54→  itemCount: number\n    55→  totalCents: number\n    56→  currency: string\n    57→  locale: string\n    58→}) {\n    59→  const { to, orderId, orderNumber, itemCount, totalCents, currency, locale } = params\n    60→\n    61→  const { brandName, brandTagline } = await getBrandConfig()\n    62→  const totalAmount = (totalCents / 100).toFixed(2)\n    63→  const currencyCode = currency.toUpperCase()\n    64→\n    65→  // Locale-aware email content\n    66→  const subjects = {\n    67→    en: `Order Confirmation #${orderNumber}`,\n    68→    es: `Confirmación de Pedido #${orderNumber}`,\n    69→    de: `Bestellbestätigung #${orderNumber}`,\n    70→  }\n    71→\n    72→  const headings = {\n    73→    en: 'Thank you for your order! 🎉',\n    74→    es: '¡Gracias por tu pedido! 🎉',\n    75→    de: 'Vielen Dank für deine Bestellung! 🎉',\n    76→  }\n    77→\n    78→  const bodies = {\n    79→    en: `Your order has been confirmed and is being processed. You will receive another email when your order ships.`,\n    80→    es: `Tu pedido ha sido confirmado y está siendo procesado. Recibirás otro correo cuando tu pedido sea enviado.`,\n    81→    de: `Deine Bestellung wurde bestätigt und wird bearbeitet. Du erhältst eine weitere E-Mail, wenn deine Bestellung versandt wird.`,\n    82→  }\n    83→\n    84→  const orderSummaryTexts = {\n    85→    en: 'Order Summary',\n    86→    es: 'Resumen del Pedido',\n    87→    de: 'Bestellübersicht',\n    88→  }\n    89→\n    90→  const itemTexts = {\n    91→    en: itemCount === 1 ? 'item' : 'items',\n    92→    es: itemCount === 1 ? 'artículo' : 'artículos',\n    93→    de: itemCount === 1 ? 'Artikel' : 'Artikel',\n    94→  }\n    95→\n    96→  const totalTexts = {\n    97→    en: 'Total',\n    98→    es: 'Total',\n    99→    de: 'Gesamt',\n   100→  }\n   101→\n   102→  const footerTexts = {\n   103→    en: 'We will send you a shipping confirmation email with tracking information as soon as your order ships.',\n   104→    es: 'Te enviaremos un correo de confirmación de envío con información de rastreo tan pronto como tu pedido sea enviado.',\n   105→    de: 'Wir senden dir eine Versandbestätigungs-E-Mail mit Tracking-Informationen, sobald deine Bestellung versandt wird.',\n   106→  }\n   107→\n   108→  const subject = subjects[locale as keyof typeof subjects] || subjects.en\n   109→  const heading = headings[locale as keyof typeof headings] || headings.en\n   110→  const body = bodies[locale as keyof typeof bodies] || bodies.en\n   111→  const orderSummaryText = orderSummaryTexts[locale as keyof typeof orderSummaryTexts] || orderSummaryTexts.en\n   112→  const itemText = itemTexts[locale as keyof typeof itemTexts] || itemTexts.en\n   113→  const totalText = totalTexts[locale as keyof typeof totalTexts] || totalTexts.en\n   114→  const footerText = footerTexts[locale as keyof typeof footerTexts] || footerTexts.en\n   115→\n   116→  try {\n   117→    const { data, error } = await resend.emails.send({\n   118→      from: `${brandName} <${process.env.RESEND_FROM_EMAIL || 'onboarding@resend.dev'}>`,\n   119→      to,\n   120→      subject,\n   121→      html: `\n   122→<!DOCTYPE html>\n   123→<html>\n   124→<head>\n   125→  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n   126→  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0\">\n   127→  <title>${subject}</title>\n   128→</head>\n   129→<body style=\"font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, 'Helvetica Neue', Arial, sans-serif; cacheDidUpdate: async () => {\n   102→              // Limit cache to 100 entries (design images can be large)\n   103→              const cache = await caches.open('fal-images')\n   104→ 128→// Clean up old cache names on activation (printify-images → printify-images-v2)\n   129→self.addEventListener('activate', (event: any) => {\n   130→  event.waitUntil(\n   131→ 136→      )\n   137→    )\n   138→  )\n   139→})\n   140→\n   141→// --- Web Push Notification Handlers (preserved from original sw.js) ---\n   142→self.addEventListener('push', (event: any) => {\n event.waitUntil(\n   154→    self.registration.showNotification(data.title || 'SKAPARA', options)\n   155→  )\n   156→})\n   157→\n   158→self.addEventListener('notificationclick', (event: any) => {\n   159→  event.notification.close()\n   160→  const url = event.notification.data?.url || '/'\n   161→  event.waitUntil(\n   162→    self.clients.matchAll({ type: 'window', includeUncontrolled: true }).then((clients: any) => {\n   163→      for (const client of clients) {\n   164→        if (client.url.includes(self.location.origin) && 'focus' in client) {\n   165→          client.navigate(url)\n   166→          return client.focus()\n   167→        }\n   168→      }\n   169→      return self.clients.openWindow(url)\n   170→    })\n   171→  )\n   172→})\n   173→\n   174→// --- Background Sync for Pending Cart Actions ---\n   175→self.addEventListener('sync', (event: any) => {\n   176→  if (event.tag === 'sync-cart') {\n   177→    event.waitUntil(syncCartActions())\n   178→  }\n   179→})\n   180→\n   181→async function syncCartActions() {\n   182→  // Get pending cart actions from IndexedDB\n   183→  const pendingActions = await getPendingCartActions()\n   184→\n   185→  for (const action of pendingActions) {\n   186→    try {\n   187→      // Replay the action\n   188→      const response = await fetch(action.url, {\n   189→        method: action.method,\n   190→        headers: action.headers,\n   191→        body: action.body,\n   192→      })\n   193→\n   194→      if (response.ok) {\n   195→        // Action succeeded, remove from pending queue\n   196→        await removePendingAction(action.id)\n   197→\n   198→        // Notify all clients that sync succeeded\n   199→        const clients = await self.clients.matchAll()\n   200→        clients.forEach((client: any) => {\n   201→          client.postMessage({\n   202→            type: 'sync-success',\n   203→            action: action.type,\n   204→          })\n   205→        })\n 214→async function getPendingCartActions(): Promise<any[]> {\n   215→  try {\n   216→    const db = await openDB()\n   217→    const tx = db.transaction('pendingActions', 'readonly')\n   218→    const store = tx.objectStore('pendingActions')\n   219→    return await store.getAll()\n   220→  } catch {\n   221→    return []\n   222→  }\n   223→}\n   224→\n   225→async function removePendingAction(id: string) {\n   226→  try {\n   227→    const db = await openDB()\n   228→    const tx = db.transaction('pendingActions', 'readwrite')\n   229→    const store = tx.objectStore('pendingActions')\n   230→    await store.delete(id)\n   231→  } catch (error) {\n   232→    console.error('Failed to remove pending action:', error)\n   233→  }\n   234→}\n   235→\n   236→function openDB(): Promise<any> {\n   237→  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n   238→    const request = indexedDB.open('pod-ai-sync', 1)\n   239→\n   240→    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error)\n   241→    request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result)\n   242→\n   243→    request.onupgradeneeded = (event: any) => {\n   244→      const db = event.target.result\n   245→      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('pendingActions')) {\n   246→        db.createObjectStore('pendingActions', { keyPath: 'id' })\n   247→      }\n   248→    }\n   249→  })\n   250→}\n {"parentUuid":"f9f2e23d-2fc5-478d-9f60-16541eb7a5bc","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project","sessionId":"e8096e0d-c27c-4885-8649-a1485c3b2f75","version":"2.1.71","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"ad0b52f0035d209ec","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MtAb5gzpGhmcsCUAjXhZDQ","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→/**\n     2→ * Email Drip Sequence System\n     3→ *\n     4→ * Schedules a series of timed emails for new users.\n     5→ * Uses drip_queue table with send_at timestamps.\n     6→ * Processed by /api/cron/drip endpoint.\n     7→ */\n     8→\n     9→import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\n    10→\n    11→const supabase = createClient(\n    12→  process.env.SUPABASE_URL || process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    13→  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY!,\n    14→  { auth: { autoRefreshToken: false, persistSession: false } }\n    15→)\n    16→\n    17→export interface DripStep {\n    18→  delay_hours: number\n    19→  template: string\n    20→  subject: string\n    21→}\n    22→\n    23→export const DRIP_SEQUENCES: Record<string, DripStep[]> = {\n    24→  welcome: [\n    25→    { delay_hours: 1, template: 'welcome', subject: 'Welcome to Skapara — Your AI Design Studio' },\n    26→    { delay_hours: 72, template: 'tips', subject: '3 Ways to Create Amazing Designs with AI' },\n    27→    { delay_hours: 168, template: 'credit_offer', subject: 'Unlock More Designs — Upgrade to Premium' },\n    28→  ],\n    29→}\n    30→\n    31→/**\n    32→ * Schedule a drip sequence for a user.\n    33→ * Inserts rows into drip_queue with calculated send_at times.\n    34→ */\n    35→export async function triggerDripSequence(\n    36→  userId: string,\n    37→  email: string,\n    38→  sequence: string\n    39→): Promise<void> {\n    40→  const steps = DRIP_SEQUENCES[sequence]\n    41→  if (!steps) {\n    42→    console.warn(`[EmailDrip] Unknown sequence: ${sequence}`)\n    43→    return\n    44→  }\n    45→\n    46→  // Check if user already has this sequence queued\n    47→  const { data: existing } = await supabase\n    48→    .from('drip_queue')\n    49→    .select('id')\n    50→    .eq('user_id', userId)\n    51→    .eq('sequence', sequence)\n    52→    .limit(1)\n    53→\n    54→  if (existing && existing.length > 0) {\n    55→    return // Already queued\n    56→  }\n    57→\n    58→  const now = new Date()\n    59→  const rows = steps.map((step, index) => {\n    60→    const sendAt = new Date(now.getTime() + step.delay_hours * 60 * 60 * 1000)\n    61→    return {\n    62→      user_id: userId,\n    63→      email,\n    64→      sequence,\n    65→      step: index,\n    66→      template: step.template,\n    67→      subject: step.subject,\n    68→      send_at: sendAt.toISOString(),\n    69→      status: 'pending',\n    70→    }\n    71→  })\n    72→\n    73→  const { error } = await supabase.from('drip_queue').insert(rows)\n    74→  if (error) {\n    75→    console.error('[EmailDrip] Failed to queue sequence:', error)\n    76→  }\n    77→}\n    78→"}]},"uuid":"a4f5d042-3e22-40ab-bd4c-f6501e024f6f","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:36:18.349Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f9f2e23d-2fc5-478d-9f60-16541eb7a5bc"}

    1→/**\n     2→ * Newsletter Drip Sequence Documentation\n     3→ *\n     4→ * GET /api/newsletter/drip-sequence-docs\n     5→ * Returns comprehensive documentation of the post-purchase drip sequence\n     6→ */\n     7→\n     8→import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n     9→import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\n    10→\n    11→const supabase = createClient(\n    12→  process.env.SUPABASE_URL!,\n    13→  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY!\n    14→)\n    15→\n    16→export async function GET(req: Request) {\n    17→  try {\n    18→    // Require internal auth (cron secret or admin token)\n    19→    const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization')\n    20→    const cronSecret = process.env.CRON_SECRET\n    21→    if (!cronSecret) {\n    22→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'CRON_SECRET not configured' }, { status: 503 })\n    23→    }\n    24→    if (!authHeader || authHeader !== `Bearer ${cronSecret}`) {\n    25→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })\n    26→    }\n    27→\n    28→    // Fetch post-purchase drip campaigns\n    29→    const { data: campaigns } = await supabase\n    30→      .from('newsletter_campaigns')\n    31→      .select('*')\n    32→      .eq('drip_sequence', 'post_purchase')\n    33→      .order('drip_step', { ascending: true })\n    34→\n    35→    const day7 = campaigns?.find(c => c.drip_step === 7)\n    36→    const day14 = campaigns?.find(c => c.drip_step === 14)\n    37→\n    38→    return NextResponse.json({\n    39→      documentation: {\n    40→        overview: 'Post-Purchase Email Drip Sequence - Automated customer follow-up after delivery',\n    41→        trigger: 'Newsletter agent PM cycle (17:00 UTC) checks orders.delivered_at timestamp',\n    42→        sequence: [\n    43→          {\n    44→            step: 'Day 7 - Satisfaction Survey',\n    45→            timing: '7 days after delivery',\n    46→            campaign_id: day7?.id,\n    47→            campaign_name: day7?.campaign_name,\n    48→            purpose: 'Gather customer feedback on order quality and experience',\n    49→            content: {\n    50→              subject_a: day7?.subject_a,\n    51→              subject_b: day7?.subject_b,\n    52→              preview_text: day7?.preview_text,\n    53→              cta_a: day7?.cta_a,\n    54→              cta_b: day7?.cta_b,\n    55→            },\n    56→            includes_survey: true,\n    57→            survey_questions: [\n    58→              'How satisfied are you with your order? (1-5 stars)',\n    59→              'How was the quality of the product?',\n    60→              'How was the shipping experience?',\n    61→              'Would you recommend Skapara to a friend?',\n    62→            ],\n    63→          },\n    64→          {\n    65→            step: 'Day 14 - Review Request',\n    66→            timing: '14 days after delivery',\n    67→            campaign_id: day14?.id,\n    68→            campaign_name: day14?.campaign_name,\n    69→            purpose: 'Encourage product reviews and social proof generation',\n    70→            content: {\n    71→              subject_a: day14?.subject_a,\n    72→              subject_b: day14?.subject_b,\n    73→              preview_text: day14?.preview_text,\n    74→              cta_a: day14?.cta_a,\n    75→              cta_b: day14?.cta_b,\n    76→            },\n    77→            includes_review_request: true,\n    78→            incentive: '10% discount on next order for leaving a review',\n    79→          },\n    80→        ],\n    81→        implementation: {\n    82→          database_table: 'newsletter_campaigns',\n    83→          drip_sequence_field: 'drip_sequence',\n    84→          drip_sequence_value: 'post_purchase',\n    85→          drip_step_field: 'drip_step',\n    86→          agent: 'newsletter',\n    87→          model: 'claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929',\n    88→          tools: ['supabase', 'resend', 'gemini'],\n    89→          schedule: '09:00 UTC (campaign creation) + 17:00 UTC (drip sequence processing)',\n    90→        },\n    91→        agent_logic: {\n    92→          description: 'Newsletter agent queries orders table during PM cycle',\n    93→          query: `\n    94→SELECT o.id, o.user_id, o.customer_email, o.locale, o.delivered_at\n    95→FROM orders o\n    96→WHERE o.status = 'delivered'\n    97→  AND o.delivered_at IS NOT NULL\n    98→  AND (\n    99→    -- Day 7 emails\n   100→    DATE(o.delivered_at) = DATE(NOW() - INTERVAL '7 days')\n   101→    OR\n   102→    -- Day 14 emails\n   103→    DATE(o.delivered_at) = DATE(NOW() - INTERVAL '14 days')\n   104→  )\n   105→          `,\n   106→          workflow: [\n   107→            '1. Query orders delivered 7 and 14 days ago',\n   108→            '2. Fetch matching drip campaigns from newsletter_campaigns',\n   109→            '3. Personalize email content with customer name and order details',\n   110→            '4. Send via Resend (respecting email preferences)',\n   111→            '5. Update newsletter_segments.md with send history',\n   112→            '6. Log sends to agent_events table',\n   113→          ],\n   114→        },\n   115→        compliance: {\n   116→          can_spam: 'All emails include unsubscribe link and physical address',\n   117→          physical_address: 'Skapara Store, Friedrichstraße 123, 10117 Berlin, Germany',\n   118→          sender: 'SKAPARA <noreply@skapara.com>',\n   119→          respect_preferences: 'Honors users.notification_preferences.email setting',\n   120→          locale_aware: 'Sends in customer preferred language (en/es/de)',\n   121→        },\n   122→        verification: {\n   123→          day7_configured: !!day7,\n   124→          day7_includes_survey: day7?.subject_a?.toLowerCase().includes('feedback') ||\n   125→                                day7?.subject_a?.toLowerCase().includes('survey') ||\n   126→                                day7?.cta_a?.toLowerCase().includes('survey') ||\n   127→                                day7?.preview_text?.toLowerCase().includes('survey'),\n   128→          day14_configured: !!day14,\n   129→          day14_includes_review_request: day14?.subject_a?.toLowerCase().includes('review') ||\n   130→                                          day14?.cta_a?.toLowerCase().includes('review'),\n   131→          all_checks_passed: (\n   132→            !!day7 &&\n   133→            !!day14 &&\n   134→            (day7?.subject_a?.toLowerCase().includes('feedback') || day7?.cta_a?.toLowerCase().includes('survey')) &&\n   135→            day14?.cta_a?.toLowerCase().includes('review')\n   136→          ),\n   137→        },\n   138→      },\n   139→    })\n   140→  } catch (error) {\n   141→    console.error('Error fetching drip sequence documentation:', error)\n   142→    return NextResponse.json(\n   143→      {\n   144→        error: 'Failed to fetch documentation',\n   145→        details: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error',\n   146→      },\n   147→      { status: 500 }\n   148→    )\n   149→  }\n   150→}\n   151→"}]},"uuid":"3a3f4635-dda4-40c7-9566-05f916f3a33d","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:36:18.601Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b8545d0d-eef7-40e9-b7e5-e8c5b0f652ed"}

return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })\n    85→  }\n    86→\n    87→  try {\n    88→    const now = new Date().toISOString()\n    89→\n    90→    // Fetch pending emails where send_at has passed\n    91→    const { data: pendingEmails, error: fetchError } = await supabase\n    92→      .from('drip_queue')\n    93→      .select('*')\n    94→      .eq('status', 'pending')\n    95→      .lte('send_at', now)\n    96→      .order('send_at', { ascending: true })\n    97→      .limit(20) // Process up to 20 per run\n    98→\n    99→    if (fetchError) {\n   100→      console.error('[Drip] Fetch error:', fetchError)\n   101→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to fetch queue' }, { status: 500 })\n   102→    }\n   103→\n   104→    if (!pendingEmails || pendingEmails.length === 0) {\n   105→      return NextResponse.json({ processed: 0 })\n   106→    }\n   107→\n   108→    let sent = 0\n   109→    let failed = 0\n   110→\n   111→    for (const item of pendingEmails) {\n   112→      try {\n   113→        const templateFn = TEMPLATES[item.template]\n   114→        if (!templateFn) {\n   115→          console.warn(`[Drip] Unknown template: ${item.template}`)\n   116→          await supabase.from('drip_queue').update({ status: 'failed' }).eq('id', item.id)\n   117→          failed++\n   118→          continue\n   119→        }\n   120→\n   121→        // GDPR compliance: Only send to confirmed newsletter subscribers\n   122→        const { data: subscriber } = await supabase\n   123→          .from('newsletter_subscribers')\n   124→ console.warn(`[Drip] Skipping unconfirmed subscriber: ${item.email}`)\n   130→          await supabase.from('drip_queue').update({ status: 'skipped' }).eq('id', item.id)\n   131→          failed++\n   132→          continue\n   133→        }\n   134→\n   135→        // Generate one-click unsubscribe token (RFC 8058)\n   136→        const unsubscribeToken = generateUnsubscribeToken(item.email)\n   137→        const unsubscribeUrl = `${BASE_URL}/api/newsletter/unsubscribe?token=${unsubscribeToken}`\n   138→\n   139→        const { html } = templateFn(item.email, unsubscribeUrl)\n   140→\n   141→        // Send via Resend\n   142→        const resendKey = process.env.RESEND_API_KEY\n   143→        if (resendKey) {\n   144→          const res = await fetch('https://api.resend.com/emails', {\n     'List-Unsubscribe': `<${unsubscribeUrl}>`,\n   150→              'List-Unsubscribe-Post': 'List-Unsubscribe=One-Click',\n   151→            },\n   152→            body: JSON.stringify({\n   153→ 157→              headers: {\n   158→                'List-Unsubscribe': `<${unsubscribeUrl}>`,\n   159→                'List-Unsubscribe-Post': 'List-Unsubscribe=One-Click',\n   160→              },\n   161→            }),\n   162→          })\n   163→\n   164→          if (!res.ok) {\n   165→            console.error(`[Drip] Resend error for ${item.id}:`, await res.text())\n   166→            await supabase.from('drip_queue').update({ status: 'failed' }).eq('id', item.id)\n   167→            failed++\n   168→            continue\n   169→          }\n   170→        }\n   171→\n   172→        // Mark as sent\n   173→        await supabase.from('drip_queue').update({\n   174→          status: 'sent',\n   175→          sent_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n   176→        }).eq('id', item.id)\n   177→\n   178→        sent++\n   179→      } catch (err) {\n   180→        console.error(`[Drip] Error processing ${item.id}:`, err)\n   181→        await supabase.from('drip_queue').update({ status: 'failed' }).eq('id', item.id)\n   182→        failed++\n   183→      }\n   184→    }\n   185→\n   186→    return NextResponse.json({ processed: pendingEmails.length, sent, failed })\n   187→  } catch (error) {\n   188→    console.error('[Drip] Cron error:', error)\n   189→    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Drip processing failed' }, {     1→/**\n     2→ * GET /api/cron/abandoned-cart-recovery\n     3→ *\n     4→ * Cron-triggered abandoned cart email processor.\n     5→ * Finds carts abandoned for >1h (first email) or >24h (second * Note: Only works for authenticated users (guest carts have no email).\n    11→ */\n    12→\n    13→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n    14→import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\n    15→import { verifyCronSecret } from '@/lib/rate-limit'\n    16→import { BASE_URL, BRAND, CONTACT } from '@/lib/store-config'\n    17→\n    18→const supabase = createClient(\n    19→  process.env.SUPABASE_URL || process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    20→  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY!,\n    21→  { auth: { autoRefreshToken: false, persistSession: false } }\n    22→)\n    23→\n    24→const CRON_SECRET = process.env.CRON_SECRET\n    25→\n    26→// Email color palette\n    27→const EMAIL_COLORS = {\n    28→  gradientStart: '#667eea',\n    29→  gradientEnd: '#764ba2',\n    30→  heading: '#667eea',\n    31→  ctaButton: '#667eea',\n    32→  bodyText: '#333',\n    33→  mutedText: '#6b7280',\n    34→  footerText: '#9ca3af',\n    35→  panelBg: '#f9fafb',\n    36→  cardBorder: '#e5e7eb',\n    37→}\n    38→\n    39→async function sendAbandonedCartEmail(params: {\n    40→  to: string\n    41→  locale: string\n    42→  cartItemCount: number\n    43→  isSecondEmail: boolean\n    44→}) {\n    45→  const { to, locale, cartItemCount, isSecondEmail } = params\n    46→\n    47→  // Locale-aware email content\n    48→  const subjects = {\n    49→    en: isSecondEmail\n    50→      ? `Your cart is still waiting! 🛒`\n    51→      : `You left ${cartItemCount} ${cartItemCount === 1 ? 'item' : 'items'} in your cart`,\n    52→    es: isSecondEmail\n    53→      ? `¡Tu carrito sigue esperando! 🛒`\n    54→      : `Dejaste ${cartItemCount} ${cartItemCount === 1 ? 'artículo' : 'artículos'} en tu carrito`,\n    55→    de: isSecondEmail\n    56→      ? `Dein Warenkorb wartet noch! 🛒`\n    57→      : `Du hast ${cartItemCount} ${cartItemCount === 1 ? 'Artikel' : 'Artikel'} im Warenkorb gelassen`,\n    58→  }\n    59→\n    60→  const headings = {\n    61→    en: isSecondEmail\n    62→      ? \"Don't miss out! 🎨\"\n    63→      : 'Did you forget something? 🛍️',\n    64→    es: isSecondEmail\n    65→      ? '¡No te lo pierdas! 🎨'\n    66→      : '¿Olvidaste algo? 🛍️',\n    67→    de: isSecondEmail\n    68→      ? 'Verpasse es nicht! 🎨'\n    69→      : 'Hast du etwas vergessen? 🛍️',\n    70→  }\n    71→\n    72→  const bodies = {\n    73→    en: isSecondEmail\n    74→      ? `Your cart with ${cartItemCount} ${cartItemCount === 1 ? 'item' : 'items'} is still waiting for you. Complete your order now and bring your designs to life!`\n    75→      : `You have ${cartItemCount} ${cartItemCount === 1 ? 'item' : 'items'} in your cart. Complete your order before ${cartItemCount === 1 ? 'it\\'s' : 'they\\'re'} gone!`,\n    76→    es: isSecondEmail\n    77→      ? `Tu carrito con ${cartItemCount} ${cartItemCount === 1 ? 'artículo' : 'artículos'} todavía te está esperando. ¡Completa tu pedido ahora y dale vida a tus diseños!`\n    78→      : `Tienes ${cartItemCount} ${cartItemCount === 1 ? 'artículo' : 'artículos'} en tu carrito. ¡Completa tu pedido antes de que ${cartItemCount === 1 ? 'desaparezca' : 'desaparezcan'}!`,\n    79→    de: isSecondEmail\n    80→      ? `Dein Warenkorb mit ${cartItemCount} ${cartItemCount === 1 ? 'Artikel' : 'Artikeln'} wartet noch auf dich. Schließe deine Bestellung jetzt ab und erwecke deine Designs zum Leben!`\n    81→      : `Du hast ${cartItemCount} ${cartItemCount === 1 ? 'Artikel' : 'Artikel'} in deinem Warenkorb. {"parentUuid":"4eef8f04-f20e-4b99-a3ac-64e0f843efaa","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project","sessionId":"e8096e0d-c27c-4885-8649-a1485c3b2f75","version":"2.1.71","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"ad0b52f0035d209ec","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KoJBkSrBg7W7df8tHfVzoc","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→/**\n     2→ * order.shipped handler — Parses shipment data from the normalized event,\n     3→ * updates the order record with tracking info, sends email notification,\n     4→ * and creates an in-app notification.\n     5→ *\n     6→ * Logic extracted from webhooks/printify/route.ts handleOrderShipped().\n     7→ */\n     8→\n     9→import type { NormalizedWebhookEvent } from '../../models'\n    10→import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\n    11→import { sendOrderShippedEmail } from '@/lib/resend'\n    12→import { findOrder, isEmailEnabled } from './utils'\n    13→\n    14→interface ShipmentData {\n    15→  carrier: string\n    16→  number: string\n    17→  url: string\n    18→}\n    19→\n    20→/**\n    21→ * Extract shipments array from normalized event data.\n    22→ * Printify: data.shipments[]\n    23→ * Printful: data.shipment (single) or data.order.shipments[]\n    24→ */\n    25→function extractShipments(data: Record<string, unknown>): ShipmentData[] {\n    26→  // Direct shipments array (Printify format)\n    27→  if (Array.isArray(data.shipments)) {\n    28→    return data.shipments as ShipmentData[]\n    29→  }\n    30→\n    31→  // Printful format: single shipment object\n    32→  if (data.shipment && typeof data.shipment === 'object') {\n    33→    const s = data.shipment as Record<string, unknown>\n    34→    return [{\n    35→      carrier: String(s.carrier || s.service || ''),\n    36→      number: String(s.tracking_number || s.number || ''),\n    37→      url: String(s.tracking_url || s.url || ''),\n    38→    }]\n    39→  }\n    40→\n    41→  // Printful nested format: data.order.shipments[]\n    42→  const orderObj = data.order as Record<string, unknown> | undefined\n    43→  if (orderObj && Array.isArray(orderObj.shipments)) {\n    44→    return (orderObj.shipments as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).map(s => ({\n    45→      carrier: String(s.carrier || s.service || ''),\n    46→      number: String(s.tracking_number || s.number || ''),\n    47→      url: String(s.tracking_url || s.url || ''),\n    48→    }))\n    49→  }\n    50→\n    51→  return []\n    52→}\n    53→\n    54→export async function handleOrderShipped(\n    55→  event: NormalizedWebhookEvent,\n    56→  supabase: SupabaseClient,\n    57→): Promise<void> {\n    58→  const order = await findOrder(event, supabase)\n    59→\n    60→  if (!order) {\n    61→    console.error(`[webhook:order.shipped] Order not found (provider: ${event.provider}, resourceId: ${event.resourceId})`)\n    62→    throw new Error('Order not found')\n    63→  }\n    64→\n    65→  const shipments = extractShipments(event.data)\n    66→\n    67→  // Build update payload\n    68→  const updateData: Record<string, unknown> = {\n    69→    status: 'shipped',\n    70→    shipped_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n    71→  }\n    72→\n    73→  if (shipments.length === 1) {\n    74→    updateData.tracking_number = shipments[0].number\n    75→    updateData.tracking_url = shipments[0].url\n    76→    updateData.carrier = shipments[0].carrier\n    77→  } else if (shipments.length > 1) {\n    78→    // Multiple shipments: concatenate tracking numbers, dedupe carriers\n    79→    updateData.tracking_number = shipments.map(s => s.number).join(', ')\n    80→    updateData.tracking_url = shipments[0].url\n    81→    updateData.carrier = shipments\n    82→      .map(s => s.carrier)\n    83→      .filter((v, i, a) => a.indexOf(v) === i)\n    84→      .join(', ')\n    85→  }\n    86→\n    87→  const primaryShipment = shipments[0] || null\n    88→\n    89→  // Update order status\n    90→  const { error } = await supabase\n    91→    .from('orders')\n    92→    .update(updateData)\n    93→    .eq('id', order.id)\n    94→\n    95→  if (error) {\n    96→    console.error('[webhook:order.shipped] Failed to update order:', error)\n    97→    throw error\n    98→  }\n    99→\n   100→  console.log(`[webhook:order.shipped] Order ${order.id} marked as shipped (provider: ${event.provider})`)\n   101→\n   102→  const orderDisplayId = order.id.slice(0, 8)\n   103→\n   104→  // Create in-app notification\n   105→  if (order.user_id) {\n   106→    const { error: notificationError } = await supabase.from('notifications').insert({\n   107→      user_id: order.user_id,\n   108→      type: 'order_shipped',\n   109→      title: `Order #${orderDisplayId} Shipped`,\n   110→      body: primaryShipment\n   111→        ? `Your order has been shipped via ${primaryShipment.carrier}. Tracking: ${primaryShipment.number}`\n   112→        : 'Your order has been shipped and is on its way!',\n   113→      data: {\n   114→        order_id: order.id,\n   115→        tracking_number: primaryShipment?.number,\n   116→        tracking_url: primaryShipment?.url,\n   117→        carrier: primaryShipment?.carrier,\n   118→      },\n   119→      is_read: false,\n   120→    })\n   121→\n   122→    if (notificationError) {\n   123→      console.error('[webhook:order.shipped] Failed to create notification:', notificationError)\n   124→    } else {\n   125→      console.log(`[webhook:order.shipped] Created notification for user ${order.user_id}`)\n   126→    }\n   127→  }\n   128→\n   129→  // Send email notification (respecting user preferences)\n   130→  if (order.customer_email && order.user_id) {\n   131→    const emailEnabled = await isEmailEnabled(order.user_id, supabase)\n   132→\n   133→    if (emailEnabled) {\n   134→      const emailResult = await sendOrderShippedEmail({\n   135→        to: order.customer_email,\n   136→        orderId: orderDisplayId,\n   137→        trackingNumber: primaryShipment?.number,\n   138→        trackingUrl: primaryShipment?.url,\n   139→        carrier: primaryShipment?.carrier,\n   140→        locale: order.locale || 'en',\n   141→      })\n   142→\n   143→      if (emailResult.success) {\n   144→        console.log(`[webhook:order.shipped] Email sent for order ${order.id}`)\n   145→      } else {\n   146→        console.error('[webhook:order.shipped] Failed to send email:', emailResult.error)\n   147→      }\n   148→    } else {\n   149→      console.log(`[webhook:order.shipped] Email skipped (user preference disabled)`)\n   150→    }\n   151→  }\n   152→\n   153→  // Audit log\n   154→  await supabase.from('audit_log').insert({\n   155→    actor_type: 'webhook',\n   156→    actor_id: `${event.provider}_webhook`,\n   157→    action: 'order_shipped',\n   158→    resource_type: 'order',\n   159→    resource_id: order.id,\n   160→    changes: updateData,\n   161→    metadata: {\n   162→      provider: event.provider,\n   163→      provider_order_id: event.resourceId,\n   164→    },\n     1→'use client'\n     2→\n     3→import React from 'react'\n     4→import DOMPurify from 'dompurify'\n     5→\n     6→/**\n     7→ * SafeHTML - XSS-protected HTML renderer\n     8→ *\n     9→ * Wraps dangerouslySetInnerHTML with DOMPurify sanitization to prevent XSS attacks.\n    10→ * All user-generated or admin-generated HTML content MUST use this component\n    11→ * instead of raw dangerouslySetInnerHTML.\n    12→ *\n    13→ * @example\n    14→ * ```tsx\n    15→ * <SafeHTML html={product.safetyInformation} className=\"prose\" />\n    16→ * ```\n    17→ */\n    18→\n    19→interface SafeHTMLProps {\n    20→  html: string\n    21→  className?: string\n    22→  /** Custom tag to render (default: 'div') */\n    23→  tag?: keyof React.JSX.IntrinsicElements\n    24→}\n    25→\n    26→export function SafeHTML({ html, className, tag = 'div' }: SafeHTMLProps) {\n    27→  // SECURITY: Sanitize HTML content to prevent XSS\n    28→  const sanitized = React.useMemo(() => {\n    29→    if (!html) return ''\n    30→\n    31→    return DOMPurify.sanitize(html, {\n    32→      // Allow common safe HTML tags\n    33→      ALLOWED_TAGS: [\n    34→        'p', 'br', 'strong', 'em', 'u', 'strike', 'code', 'pre',\n    35→        'a', 'ul', 'ol', 'li', 'blockquote', 'h1', 'h2', 'h3', 'h4', 'h5', 'h6',\n    36→        'table', 'thead', 'tbody', 'tr', 'th', 'td', 'span', 'div',\n    37→        'img', 'sup', 'sub', 'abbr', 'cite', 'q', 'kbd', 'samp', 'var',\n    38→      ],\n    39→      // Allow safe attributes only\n    40→      ALLOWED_ATTR: [\n    41→        'href', 'title', 'target', 'rel', 'alt', 'src', 'width', 'height',\n    42→        'class', 'id', 'aria-label', 'aria-hidden', 'role',\n    43→      ],\n    44→      // Allow only safe URL protocols\n    45→      ALLOWED_URI_REGEXP: /^(?:(?:(?:f|ht)tps?|mailto|tel):|[^a-z]|[a-z+.\\-]+(?:[^a-z+.\\-:]|$))/i,\n    46→      // Disallow data: URIs and javascript: URIs\n    47→      FORBID_TAGS: ['script', 'style', 'iframe', 'object', 'embed', 'form', 'input', 'button'],\n    48→      FORBID_ATTR: ['onerror', 'onload', 'onclick', 'onmouseover', 'onfocus', 'onblur'],\n    49→    })\n    50→  }, [html])\n    51→\n    52→  const Tag = tag as any\n    53→\n    54→  return (\n    55→    <Tag\n    56→      className={className}\n    57→      dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: sanitized }}\n    58→    />\n    59→  )\n    60→}\n    61→"}]},"uuid":"70fb0aae-ffd8-4341-a9ce-2e7f011fed7b","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:36:41.957Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"25d39f94-a4f9-4980-b618-a697715ed38f"}
